- **inception**: Turn behavioral rules for AI agents into the higher-level concept they derive from. Rule lists ('do this, don't do that') invite letter-compliance and cover only anticipated cases; this skill finds the principle that makes the desired behavior the natural one, states it overtly and positively with its reasons, and keeps only 2–3 rules as derived anchors. Use when writing or revising any agent instructions (system prompts, AGENTS.md, skills, subagent definitions), when the same correction keeps getting repeated to an agent, when an agent follows the letter of a rule but misses its intent, or o… ([hgkahng/skills](https://github.com/hgkahng/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/inception))
- **inception**: Lean Inception, lite - one focused pass producing a vision, personas, an is/is not/does/does not table, and an MVP sequence. Writes the first draft of prd.md. ([mauriciovieira/groundwork](https://github.com/mauriciovieira/groundwork/tree/HEAD/skills/inception))
- **inception**: Run a separate Inception (discovery) ledger: pin a frame, declare checkable criteria/questions/units, optionally challenge, seal an operational handoff under .lider/handoffs/. RECOMMENDED before /pipeline construction; required only in strict mode. Use when starting non-trivial work, clarifying scope, or /inception. ([T50-Systems/lider](https://github.com/T50-Systems/lider/tree/HEAD/plugins/lider/skills/inception))
- **inception-application-design**: Design application components, methods, services, and dependencies at a high level. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-application-design) · ★1)
- **inception-from-code**: Use when adopting the kit into an EXISTING repository (brownfield), to draft the Inception artifacts from the code instead of from blank templates. The agent reads the repository and writes five drafts, only under docs/inception-draft/, each tagged by how each fact is known; a person reviews them and moves what is right into place. Agents propose, humans ratify. ([SeaBrad72/sparkwright](https://github.com/SeaBrad72/sparkwright/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-from-code) · ★3 · 3 copies)
- **inception-planning**: Guidelines for planning, requirements analysis, and design phases of development work ([JanMichaelSE/agentic-marketplace](https://github.com/JanMichaelSE/agentic-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/engineering/skills/inception-planning) · ★1)
- **inception-requirements**: Gather and analyze requirements with adaptive depth. Creates requirement questions and generates requirements document. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-requirements) · ★1)
- **inception-reverse-engineering**: Analyze existing codebase for brownfield projects. Generates architecture, API, component, and technology documentation. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-reverse-engineering) · ★1)
- **inception-scrub**: Use when the Operator asks to discuss or process an INCEPTION_SCRUB finding, or when assessing or correcting semantic drift, OBE, superseded, incorrect, contradictory, duplicate, or unsupported inception material. Guides finding discussion through disposition and explicit Approve confirmation of the existing scrub command. Source maintenance only; does not consolidate or admit Canon. ([rhodiusjeff/PokerNight](https://github.com/rhodiusjeff/PokerNight/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/inception-scrub))
- **inception-units-generation**: Decompose system into units of work. Two-part stage with planning and generation phases. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-units-generation) · ★1)
- **inception-user-stories**: Create user stories and personas from requirements. Two-part stage with planning and generation phases. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-user-stories) · ★1)
- **inception-workflow-planning**: Create execution plan determining which stages to run. Analyzes scope, impact, and risk to recommend optimal workflow. ([yamatatsu/claude-aidlc](https://github.com/yamatatsu/claude-aidlc/tree/HEAD/skills/inception-workflow-planning) · ★1)
- **inception-workshop**: Facilitate Lean Inception workshops through 8 structured steps. LOAD THIS SKILL when user mentions: inception workshop, lean inception, product vision, validate the product vision, validate vision, tradeoffs board, personas, empathy map, brainstorming, user journey mapping, feature sequencing, MVP canvas, product discovery, validate MVP, validate tradeoffs, validate personas, check inception step, review product vision, or validate inception. Executes facilitation, batch generation, tradeoff analysis, and validation modes. ([faparicior/sdlc-skills](https://github.com/faparicior/sdlc-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/discovery/inception-workshop) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **inception:clarify**: Assumption refinement tool. Use when the developer wants to revisit, challenge, or update inferences Jordan made during discovery. Can be run at any point — post-discovery, mid-development, after Alex's research returns new signals, or after a prototype reveals something unexpected. Updates the living spec and assumptions log in place. Trigger phrases: "revisit assumptions", "refine requirements", "update the spec", "Jordan inferred wrong", "I want to change X", "the prototype showed something different", "rethink the scope". ([c0dezer019/project-inception](https://github.com/c0dezer019/project-inception/tree/HEAD/skills/inception%3Aclarify))
- **inception:design**: This skill should be used after project discovery is complete and the user is ready to design the architecture. Trigger phrases include: "design the architecture", "pick a stack", "design phase", "what folder structure should I use", "help me design the database", "inception design", "architecture phase". Also triggers automatically after inception:discovery completes. Expects a discovery context object — if one is not present, prompt the user to run inception:discovery first or provide project details manually. ([c0dezer019/project-inception](https://github.com/c0dezer019/project-inception/tree/HEAD/skills/inception%3Adesign))
- **inception:discovery**: This skill should be used when the user wants to start a new project from scratch, plan a project, or kick off a development effort. Trigger phrases include: "start a new project", "let's plan X", "I want to build X", "project inception", "discovery phase", "help me scope a project", "I have an idea for X". Also trigger proactively when the user describes a project idea but hasn't established any scope, audience, or requirements yet. Run this skill before inception:design. ([c0dezer019/project-inception](https://github.com/c0dezer019/project-inception/tree/HEAD/skills/inception%3Adiscovery))
- **inception:feature-plan**: This skill should be used when the user wants to plan a new feature for an existing project that was previously designed with the project-inception plugin. Trigger phrases include: "plan a new feature", "add X to the project", "feature planning", "integrate X", "I want to add X", "what would it take to add X", "feature inception", "plan feature integration". Also triggers when the user describes a new capability they want to add to an existing codebase. Expects an existing project — run inception:discovery and inception:design first for new projects. ([c0dezer019/project-inception](https://github.com/c0dezer019/project-inception/tree/HEAD/skills/inception%3Afeature-plan))
- **inception_architect**: Architecture evaluation — validate design against established patterns. ([vig-os/scitadel](https://github.com/vig-os/scitadel/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/inception_architect) · ★3 · 4 copies)
- **inception_explore**: Divergent exploration — understand the problem space before jumping to solutions. ([vig-os/scitadel](https://github.com/vig-os/scitadel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/inception_explore) · ★3 · 4 copies)

- **inception_scope**: Convergent scoping — define what to build and what not to build. ([vig-os/scitadel](https://github.com/vig-os/scitadel/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/inception_scope) · ★3 · 4 copies)

- **incheon-airport**: 仁川国際空港の旅客便の到着・出発状況(遅延・ゲート・ターミナル)に加え、空港バスの路線・始発/終電・運賃と出国場の混雑度を仁川国際空港公社のAPIで調べる。空港鉄道(AREX)のデータも併記。無料APIキーが必要。 ([tahodev/tabi-skill](https://github.com/tahodev/tabi-skill/tree/HEAD/incheon-airport) · ★1)
- **inchi-annotation-counting**: Use when you have an mzmine-processed FBMN feature table with MS/MS spectra and need to benchmark how many features get an InChI from GNPS strict vs iterative weighted analog search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-annotation-counting))
- **inchi-generation-from-structures**: Use when raw natural-product structure tables (e.g. smiles.tsv.gz, nominal.tsv.gz) hold SMILES identifiers or compound names, and a standardized InChI column is required for downstream harmonization or database upload. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-generation-from-structures))
- **inchi-key-based-structure-unification**: Use when curating or merging chemical structure collections from multiple provenance where duplicate/same-core molecules must be de-duplicated into one canonical entry while retaining stereochemical variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-key-based-structure-unification))
- **inchi-key-molecule-deduplication**: Use when preparing query and reference libraries for spectral database lookup: overlapping molecules between sides would inflate recall, so InChI-key-based disjointness must be asserted before ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-key-molecule-deduplication))
- **inchi-smiles-annotation-checking**: Use when imported MS/MS spectra (MGF, msp, mzML, mzXML, JSON) carry inconsistent, wrongly labeled, or missing structure annotations (InChI, SMILES, InChIKey) or ionmode/adduct/charge fields that must be cleaned before scoring. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-smiles-annotation-checking))
- **inchi-to-smiles-structure-normalization**: Use when reference .msp blocks lack precomputed Fingerprint rows and contain only InChI: entries (or SMILES needing canonicalization), and dash-separated bit-location targets are required for IDSL_MINT transformer decoder training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchi-to-smiles-structure-normalization))
- **inchidere-zi-casa**: Închidere automată de zi configurabilă pe unitate, casa provizorie, auditul numerarului și corectarea zilelor deja închise. Pentru „închide ziua”, „verifică închiderile”, „modifică numărarea”, „raportul Z nu bate cu POS”, „predarea nu apare” sau „schimbă ora închiderii”. ([sinesteziadrimo/symbaiposmcp](https://github.com/sinesteziadrimo/symbaiposmcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/symbai-core/skills/inchidere-zi-casa))
- **inchikey-annotation-aggregation**: Use when you have matched annotations from a spectra search (e.g. CSA spectra searched against an .msp library) on an aligned peak table and need to consolidate them per compound (InChIKey) and identify annotations beyond those recovered by MS/MS library searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-annotation-aggregation))
- **inchikey-annotation-based-spectrum-selection**: Use when a cleaned MS/MS library must be split into training spectra with structural identifiers (InChIKeys) and query spectra without them, or when a deduplicated per-molecule reference set is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-annotation-based-spectrum-selection))
- **inchikey-based-compound-grouping**: Use when an MGF-format MS/MS library (e.g. MassSpecGym.mgf) must be grouped by underlying compound identity to compute intra-compound spectral similarity or de-duplicate spectra before model training or benchmarking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-compound-grouping))
- **inchikey-based-database-querying**: Use when you hold a merged metabolite table keyed on unique InChIKeys and need to populate per-metabolite attributes (name descriptors, MW, formula, database IDs) for downstream interstudy comparability checks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-database-querying))
- **inchikey-based-dataset-merging**: Use when multiple metabolomics datasets with heterogeneous metabolite identifiers (names, HMDB, KEGG, PubChem CIDs, CAS) must be merged and intercompared, and a systematic InChIKey-based harmonization with curation is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-dataset-merging))
- **inchikey-based-spectral-lookup**: Use when building an in-house m/z–RT metabolite standard library in metScribeR and the standards dataframe contains an inchiKey column, so public MS/MS spectra from MoNA should be merged into the exported library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-spectral-lookup))
- **inchikey-based-spectrum-holdout-splitting**: Use when building or benchmarking a spectral similarity model (Spec2Vec, cosine) on GNPS-style MS/MS libraries and you must guarantee that query compounds never appear in the training library, i.e. unknown-compound (structural-analogue) search evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-spectrum-holdout-splitting))
- **inchikey-based-structure-matching**: Use when benchmarking structure prediction or annotation pipelines where candidate and reference structures must be compared exactly at the connectivity level, e.g., scoring BAM derivatives against known suspects. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-based-structure-matching))
- **inchikey-block-comparison**: Use when a candidate list retrieved from PubChem, ChemSpider, KEGG, or a user-supplied SDF contains stereoisomeric duplicates (shared first InChIKey block) that would otherwise inflate candidate counts and split score mass across chemically equivalent entries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-block-comparison))
- **inchikey-canonical-smiles-resolution**: Use when building a per-compound dataset from many spectra entries and you must deduplicate compounds and standardize structures (e.g. resolving canonical SMILES from InChIKeys before merging MS/MS spectra). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-canonical-smiles-resolution))
- **inchikey-canonicalization-verification**: Use when a compound database ships structure files (.mol/.cdxml) plus identifier columns that must be regenerated and validated, or when checking whether deposited SMILES/InChI/InChIKey fields are populated and canonical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-canonicalization-verification))
- **inchikey-compound-deduplication**: Use when merging MS/MS spectral reference libraries (NIST20, Agilent METLIN, GNPS, MSDIAL) and you need to quantify and remove cross-library duplicate compounds before building a combined database or reference file. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-compound-deduplication))
- **inchikey-compound-identifier-handling**: Reference for pipelines that must merge MS/MS spectra from the same compound across collision energies or align predicted fingerprints with candidate compound lists drawn from MetaboQuest/PubChem. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-compound-identifier-handling))
- **inchikey-connectivity-block-label-matching**: Use when you need ground-truth same/different-compound labels to benchmark spectral similarity scores (cosine, entropy) on MS/MS library pairs, and both spectra carry InChIKey annotations plus precursor m/z metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-connectivity-block-label-matching))
- **inchikey-derivation-from-chemical-identifiers**: Use when MS/MS library spectra (e.g. GNPS ALL_GNPS.json, 154,820 spectra) lack InChIKey annotations but carry compound name, molecular formula, or parent mass that can be resolved against PubChem. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-derivation-from-chemical-identifiers))
- **inchikey-exact-match-accuracy-computation**: Use when evaluating de novo structure elucidation output (SELFIES/SMILES candidates from a spectrum-conditioned model) and you need the fraction of predictions whose full InChIKey string exactly equals the reference molecule's InChIKey. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-exact-match-accuracy-computation))
- **inchikey-exact-match-accuracy-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking de novo structure elucidation from mass spectra on MassSpecGym/NPLIB1 test folds and you need Top-1/Top-10 exact-identity accuracy over full InChIKey strings for generated molecule sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-exact-match-accuracy-evaluation))
- **inchikey-extraction-from-spectral-library**: Use when you have a GNPS DBResult file from spectral library matching that lacks ClassyFire superclass, class, and subclass columns, and you need to restore chemical ontology annotations by submitting compound identifiers to external batch classification services for re-annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/inchikey-extraction-from-spectral-library))
- **inchikey-identifier-normalization**: Use when gNPS has stopped supplying ClassyFire ontology information for spectral library matches (as of the ConCISE documentation snapshot) and you need to manually retrieve chemical classifications. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/inchikey-identifier-normalization))
- **inchikey-identifier-overlap-analysis**: Use when integrating two or more mass spectral libraries (e.g., .MGF, .MSP, .JSON exports) and you need the fraction of compounds present in multiple sources, per-library compound/spectrum counts, and single-library exclusivity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-identifier-overlap-analysis))
- **inchikey-molecule-indexing**: Use when annotated MS/MS spectra (InChIKey/SMILES metadata) must be grouped at the compound level, e.g. to prevent spectra of the same molecule from leaking across model train/validation/test splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-molecule-indexing))
- **inchikey-normalization-and-deduplication**: Use when you have an annotated MS/MS spectral dataset with structure metadata (InChI or SMILES records) linked to InChIKey identifiers, and you observe that some InChIKeys are associated with multiple or variant InChI strings due to curation inconsistencies, stereoisomerism notation differences, or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/inchikey-normalization-and-deduplication) · 2 copies)
- **inchikey-protonation-layer-interpretation**: Use when a merged metabolite table contains multiple InChIKeys that share the same 14-character molecular-skeleton prefix (e.g. palmitate vs. palmitic acid) and entries must be reduced to one non-conjugated compound per pair. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-protonation-layer-interpretation))
- **inchikey-scaffold-dataset-split-evaluation**: Use when benchmarking C2MS (compound-to-spectrum) models like FraGNNet, ICEBERG, GrAFF-MS, MassFormer, NEIMS, or Precursor-Only on MS/MS prediction or retrieval tasks and you need generalization-controlled splits with spectral similarity metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-scaffold-dataset-split-evaluation))
- **inchikey-set-difference-analysis**: Use when you have InChIKey or SMILES lists from a new library and several existing open/commercial spectral libraries and need to quantify which compounds are novel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-set-difference-analysis))
- **inchikey-short-key-structure-matching**: Use when building a benchmarking or dereplication set that must join a curated structure table (e.g. DNP-derived) to an MS/MS spectral library (e.g. GNPS-derived) at the level of unique parent structures rather than individual stereoisomers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-short-key-structure-matching))
- **inchikey-skeleton-matching**: Use when building a ground-truth set of known BGC–spectrum links: you have MIBiG BGC records with structural annotations and GNPS library spectra with InChIKey annotations, but no direct spectral link, and need a chirality-insensitive structural join. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-skeleton-matching))
- **inchikey-skeleton-prefix-matching**: Use when merging metabolite datasets that report heterogeneous non-systematic identifiers and need a systematic key to collapse conjugated base/acid pairs or duplicate entries before manual curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-skeleton-prefix-matching))
- **inchikey-smiles-compound-deduplication**: Use when a curated MS/MS library needs unique-compound counts for reporting or validation, and each spectrum carries structure metadata (InChIKey or SMILES) that can serve as the de-duplication key. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-smiles-compound-deduplication))
- **inchikey-smiles-standardization**: Use when when you have raw MS/MS spectra from repositories like GNPS that lack or have inconsistent chemical structure annotations (InChI/SMILES), and you need to produce a curated dataset with uniform 14-character InChIKey and SMILES/InChI annotations for downstream machine learning or similarity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/inchikey-smiles-standardization) · 2 copies)
- **inchikey-stereoisomer-filtering**: Use when candidate lists retrieved from PubChem, ChemSpider or KEGG contain multiple stereoisomers sharing a first InChIKey block, and scores must be assigned per unique connectivity before ranking Top 1/3/5. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/inchikey-stereoisomer-filtering))
- **inchikey-structural-similarity-computation**: Use when you have a ranked list of library candidates (top 2000 by MS2Deepscore) from MS/MS spectral matching and need to re-rank them using structural metadata to distinguish true analogues and exact matches from false positives. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/inchikey-structural-similarity-computation) · 2 copies)
- **inchikey-structural-similarity-scoring**: Use when you have candidate library matches from MS2Deepscore ranking (top 2000 spectra per query) with InChIKey annotations, and need to quantify structural similarity between query and candidate compounds to inform downstream match ranking and filtering by MS2Query's random forest model.
